## Acceptance Criteria

Every criterion is a Given/When/Then a QA tester can pass or fail without reading the rest of this document. Rule ids (SL-n), state-machine ids (T-n), formula ids (F-n), Open Question ids (OQ-n) and invariant ids (I-n) point at this document; kernel, Graph and Controller facts are cited by rule id or section name (CR-8, CR-15, CR-18, D.10 – D.12, the kernel's G4 acceptance criterion, Graph LS-21, Controller PC-17), never by line number, because line numbers drift on every edit of the owning GDD. The ids below are **stable labels, not a reading order**: criteria added after the first draft keep a letter or a higher number inside the group where they belong.

**Tags.** **(auto)** automated and deterministic — no wall clock, no real disk, no unseeded randomness unless the line says otherwise; **(lint)** a static or data-file check run in CI; **(review)** a named reviewer reads an artefact; **(manual)** a person plays or inspects and a retained screenshot or sheet is the evidence; **(spike)** measured on the target machine by the concept's "save round-trip with version" spike, which replaces every [UNV] tag. Test locations follow the testing standard: `tests/unit/save-load/` (pure functions over in-memory byte arrays and the injected seams below), `tests/integration/save-load/` (a real temporary directory with fault injection), `tests/fixtures/saves/v1/` (immutable golden saves), `production/qa/evidence/` (retained screenshots, sheets and spike measurements).

**Fixture constants (the GDD defaults; every example below uses them).** `TICK_RATE` 60; `AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_TICKS` 10 800; `AUTOSAVE_MIN_GAP_TICKS` 1 800; `AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS` 300; `AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS` 7 200; `DEATH_LOOP_WINDOW_TICKS` 3 600; `AUTOSAVE_GENERATIONS` 3; `AUTOSAVE_FAIL_BACKOFF_MAX_TICKS` 4 800; `AUTOSAVE_FAIL_ESCALATE_COUNT` 3; `DOWN_TO_DEATH_TICKS` 1 800; `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 (600 ticks); `QUARANTINE_AFTER_FAILURES` 2; `MAX_SAVE_BYTES` 64 MiB; `MAX_BLOCK_BYTES` 32 MiB; `MAX_HEADER_BYTES` 64 KiB; `MAX_BLOCKS` 128 (kernel GDD value); `MAX_DECODE_DEPTH` 16; `MAX_DECODE_NODES` 250 000; `MAX_JSON_DEPTH` 8; `SAVE_QUIT_TIMEOUT_MS` 5 000; `RENAME_RETRY_COUNT` 3; `RENAME_RETRY_DELAY_MS` 50; `LOAD_MAX_ATTEMPTS` 3 (= `AUTOSAVE_GENERATIONS`); `SAVE_SIZE_ALARM_BYTES` 2 MiB; `LOADING_TARGET_MS` 1 000.

**Harness seams (injected, so a test needs no wall clock, no real disk and no randomness).** A tick source (`T`); a file-system adapter with fault injection at each write state of T-4; a registry view; a kernel control-plane spy (records `request_snapshot`, `request_restore`, `request_shutdown`); a Graph `failures()` stand-in (LS-14 descriptor fixtures; the real Graph read view where `graph.state` is registered) and an Attention SIM view stand-in (shaped as the designed `attention.view`); a `release_mode` flag; a `halt` handler; an entropy source (for `campaign_id`); an allocation spy; and the seed constants `FUZZ_SEED`, `LOSS_FUZZ_SEED`, `INVARIANT_FUZZ_SEED`, `BLOCK_FUZZ_SEED`, each stored in the fixture file and printed on failure. **Stand-in blocks** are the kernel acceptance fixture's blocks, as the kernel's own G4 criterion uses them.

**Glossary.** *Generation*: one complete self-verifying save file. *Ring*: the last `AUTOSAVE_GENERATIONS` generations. *`RISKY`*: written while a soft gate condition was closed. *Demoted*: stepped back from, kept on disk as `gen_k.demoted`, never a default candidate. *Quarantine*: files that failed as CORRUPT, RESTORE_FAILED or STALE_EPOCH — never auto-deleted. *EXACT / REBASED*: whether the post-restore hash compare can prove equality (F-4). *Re-signed*: a mutated fixture whose trailer and block hashes were recomputed so it reaches the later pre-check steps.

**Pre-check coverage.** Every closed `SL_PRECHECK_*` reason of T-5 has at least one deterministic positive test; the fuzz (AC-SL-13) never counts as coverage.

| T-5 step (by name) | Reason(s) | Criterion |
|---|---|---|
| open and read | `FILE_UNREADABLE` (transient, not quarantined) | AC-SL-98 |
| size floor, `MAX_SAVE_BYTES` | `FILE_TOO_SMALL`, `FILE_TOO_LARGE` | AC-SL-10 |
| 7-byte magic prefix | `BAD_MAGIC` | AC-SL-10 |
| trailer SHA-256 | `FILE_SHA_MISMATCH` | AC-SL-10, -11, -12 |
| layout id | `LAYOUT_UNKNOWN` (INCOMPATIBLE) | AC-SL-15, -99 |
| `header_len`, JSON parse | `HEADER_LEN_INVALID`, `HEADER_NOT_JSON` | AC-SL-99 |
| header fields | `HEADER_FIELD_MISSING`, `_TYPE`, `_NOT_INT`, `_HEX_MALFORMED` | AC-SL-02, -03, -100, -96 |
| `format_version`, `codec` | `FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED` | AC-SL-12, -15, -101 |
| `min_reader_version`, `tick_rate` | `MIN_READER_EXCEEDED`, `TICK_RATE_MISMATCH` | AC-SL-15 |
| block table | `BLOCK_TABLE_INVALID`, `PAYLOAD_LENGTH_MISMATCH`, `BLOCK_TOO_LARGE`, `BLOCK_COUNT_EXCEEDS_MAX`, `DUPLICATE_BLOCK_ID` | AC-SL-19, -102 |
| per-block `payload_sha256` | `BLOCK_HASH_MISMATCH` (names the block) | AC-SL-103 |
| registered or retired | `UNKNOWN_BLOCK`, `RETIRED_BLOCK_STRIPPED` | AC-SL-15, -17 |
| class and scope | `PERCEPTION_BLOCK_PRESENT`, `SCOPE_MISMATCH` | AC-SL-18 |
| `schema_version` ∈ `supported_versions` and migration chain | `SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED` | AC-SL-15, -104 |
| decode and type walk | `BLOCK_DECODE_FAILED`, `BLOCK_TYPE_FORBIDDEN`, `BLOCK_DECODE_LIMITS` | AC-SL-05, -07, -105 |
| clock block and header cross-checks | `CLOCK_BLOCK_INVALID`, `HEADER_CLOCK_MISMATCH` | AC-SL-19, -105 |
| mandatory set | `MANDATORY_MISSING` | AC-SL-17, -70, -106 |
| seats, campaign | `SEATS_INVALID`, `CAMPAIGN_MISMATCH` | AC-SL-19, -107 |
| step order | first failing step wins | AC-SL-97 |
| reproduction stage (after restore) | `VERIFY_NOT_REPRODUCED`, `VERIFY_WINDOW_MISSED` | AC-SL-59, -60 |

### A. Container, interchange and trust (SL-1, SL-2, F-10, S-1)

1. **AC-SL-01 (auto) — S-1 fixture, bit-exact.** GIVEN a fixture with `world_seed = 2^64 − 1`, `T = 2^53 + 1`, and a Graph-shaped stand-in block holding `PackedInt64Array` and `PackedByteArray` fields, WHEN a generation is written and read back, THEN the header carries `world_seed` as `"ffffffffffffffff"` and `T` as `"0020000000000001"` (16 lowercase hex characters), the restored `kernel.clock` and every packed field are bit-identical, and the header contains no JSON number above 2^53. Cites: SL-1, SL-2, F-10.
2. **AC-SL-02 (auto) — hex is exact-width.** GIVEN headers whose `T` is `"FFFFFFFFFFFFFFFF"`, `"ffffffffffffffff0"`, `"ffffffffffffff"` or `"gggggggggggggggg"`, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN each is refused `SL_PRECHECK_HEADER_HEX_MALFORMED` and the kernel spy records zero calls. Cites: F-10, T-5.
3. **AC-SL-03 (auto) — numbers stay numbers below 2^53.** GIVEN a re-signed header whose `generation` is `9007199254740993` or `1.5`, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN it is refused `SL_PRECHECK_HEADER_FIELD_NOT_INT`. Cites: SL-1, S-1.
4. **AC-SL-04 (auto) — layout and trailer law.** GIVEN a generation file, WHEN its last 32 bytes are compared with SHA-256 over `[0, size − 32)`, THEN they are equal for every file the writer produces, for layout-id value 1 and for a synthetic future layout-id value. Cites: SL-1 (format invariant).
5. **AC-SL-05 (auto) — objects are never decoded.** GIVEN a payload containing an `Object` marker, a `Callable`, a `Resource` path or nesting beyond `MAX_DECODE_DEPTH`, WHEN the pre-check decodes it, THEN it is refused (`SL_PRECHECK_BLOCK_TYPE_FORBIDDEN` or `_BLOCK_DECODE_LIMITS`), zero objects are instantiated, and the kernel spy records zero calls. Cites: SL-2, T-5.
6. **AC-SL-06 (lint) — no object-allowing decode or Resource path.** GIVEN the Save/Load source, WHEN searched, THEN no object-allowing decode call, no `ResourceLoader` or `.tres` path and no `load()` of a user file appears. Recommended gate level BLOCKING (guards the object-injection boundary). Cites: SL-2.
7. **AC-SL-07 (auto) — type walk, both directions.** GIVEN a snapshot containing only null, bool, int, float, String, Array, Dictionary (keys `String` or `int`) and `PackedByteArray`, `PackedInt32Array`, `PackedInt64Array`, `PackedFloat32Array`, `PackedFloat64Array`, `PackedStringArray`, WHEN the type walk runs, THEN it is accepted. GIVEN snapshots containing, one at a time, `Vector2i`, `Vector2`, `Vector3`, `Color`, `Rect2`, `NodePath`, `RID`, `Callable`, `Signal`, `StringName` as a value, a `Dictionary` with a float key, or a `Dictionary` with a `Vector2i` key, THEN each is refused `SL_PRECHECK_BLOCK_TYPE_FORBIDDEN`. GIVEN nesting depth `MAX_DECODE_DEPTH` (16) and then 17, THEN depth 16 is accepted and 17 is refused `SL_PRECHECK_BLOCK_DECODE_LIMITS`; GIVEN `MAX_DECODE_NODES` (250 000) nodes and then 250 001, THEN the same boundary holds; a single `PackedInt64Array` of 10 000 000 elements counts as one node and is accepted if within `MAX_BLOCK_BYTES`. GIVEN boot fixtures with `StringName` keys or a typed container, THEN boot fails `SL_BOOT_STRINGNAME_KEY` / `SL_BOOT_TYPED_CONTAINER`. Cites: SL-2, SL-15 items 3 and 12.
8. **AC-SL-08 (auto + lint) — the dev dump is not a load path.** GIVEN the dev `dump-save` command, WHEN it renders a block, THEN 64-bit values appear as strings (auto); AND GIVEN the source, THEN the dump module is not referenced from the load pipeline and is excluded from release builds (lint). Cites: SL-2.
9. **AC-SL-09 (auto) — file bytes are not canonical, state equality is.** GIVEN two saves of identical state whose dictionaries were built in different insertion orders, WHEN compared, THEN the file bytes may differ and `state_hash` is equal. Cites: SL-1, kernel D.12.
96. **AC-SL-96 (auto) — `hex64` round trip, 64- and 128-bit.** GIVEN x in {0, 1, 2^53 − 1, 2^53, 2^53 + 1, 2^63 − 1, 2^63, 2^64 − 1} and a 128-bit `campaign_id` of `0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef`, WHEN `hex64`/`unhex64` (and the 32-character variant) run, THEN every value round-trips bit for bit, the strings are exactly 16 (or 32) lowercase hex characters, and a signed GDScript read of 2^63 and above is negative yet round-trips. A `campaign_id` of 31 or 33 characters, or uppercase, is refused `SL_PRECHECK_HEADER_HEX_MALFORMED`. Cites: F-10, SL-1.
96f. **AC-SL-96f (auto) — payload separability.** GIVEN a generation, WHEN only the payload region is sliced out using the header's block table and passed to a block decoder, THEN every block restores without the wrapper (the shape a VS-B guest transfer needs). Cites: SL-1, SL-17.
100. **AC-SL-100 (auto) — header field table.** GIVEN re-signed headers each missing one required key (every key in the SL-1 table, one run each) or carrying a wrong type, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN `SL_PRECHECK_HEADER_FIELD_MISSING` / `_TYPE` names the key; GIVEN an added unknown key with `min_reader_version` satisfied, THEN the file loads; GIVEN a corrupt `block_state_hash` with everything else valid, THEN it loads and a diagnostic is logged; GIVEN a `PROFILE` file with no `state_hash`, THEN it loads; GIVEN any file the writer produces, THEN `min_reader_version == format_version`. Cites: SL-1, SL-16.

### B. Pre-check, failure classes and fuzz (SL-11, T-5)

10. **AC-SL-10 (auto) — damaged-file table.** GIVEN the golden v1 generation and these fixed inputs (all derived from it; no random source), WHEN the pre-check runs on each, THEN the reason and class are exactly as listed, the kernel spy records zero calls, and the loader falls back to the next candidate: (a) 0 bytes → `SL_PRECHECK_FILE_TOO_SMALL`; (b) truncated to 11, 12 and 45 bytes → `SL_PRECHECK_FILE_TOO_SMALL`; (c) truncated to 46 bytes and to `size − 1` → `SL_PRECHECK_FILE_SHA_MISMATCH`; (d) a 46-byte file of zero bytes → `SL_PRECHECK_BAD_MAGIC`; (e) golden bytes with the first 7 bytes replaced and the trailer recomputed → `SL_PRECHECK_BAD_MAGIC`; (f) a file of `MAX_SAVE_BYTES + 1` bytes → `SL_PRECHECK_FILE_TOO_LARGE`; (g) a valid file of exactly `MAX_SAVE_BYTES` bytes is not refused for size. All of (a)–(f) are class CORRUPT and quarantine candidates. The size floor is 46 bytes (12 + 2 + 32). Cites: T-5 size floor, magic, trailer.
11. **AC-SL-11 (auto) — a flipped bit in the layout-id byte is CORRUPT.** GIVEN an intact generation with one bit flipped in the layout-id byte, and an intact older generation, WHEN the loader runs, THEN the flipped file is `SL_PRECHECK_FILE_SHA_MISMATCH` (CORRUPT), not `SL_PRECHECK_LAYOUT_UNKNOWN`, it is a quarantine candidate, and the older generation loads. Cites: SL-1 format invariant, T-5 (trailer before layout id).
12. **AC-SL-12 (auto) — a flipped bit in `format_version` is not "newer build".** GIVEN an intact generation with one bit flipped inside the header's `format_version`, WHEN the loader runs, THEN the outcome is CORRUPT and a fallback, never `SL_PRECHECK_FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED`. Cites: SL-11, T-5 order.
13. **AC-SL-13 (auto) — 1 000-mutation fuzz: a refusal never reaches the kernel, nothing crashes.** GIVEN `FUZZ_MUTATION_COUNT` (1 000) mutations generated from the constant seed `FUZZ_SEED` (about 125 per class: payload bit flip; header bit flip; truncation; corrupt `header_len`; re-signed header edit; re-signed payload replacement with a forbidden-type tree; random-byte replacement; oversize length fields), each tagged by the generator `MUST_REFUSE` or `METADATA_ONLY` (metadata-only: edits confined to `created_unix`, `game_version`, `build_id`, `catalogue_hash`, `trigger`, or an added unknown header key, all re-signed), WHEN each mutated file is loaded, THEN (i) every `MUST_REFUSE` file is refused with exactly one reason from the closed `SL_PRECHECK_*` set, the kernel spy records zero calls and no object is instantiated; (ii) every `METADATA_ONLY` file is accepted and its decoded `state_hash` equals the golden file's; (iii) no mutation produces an exception, a crash or a third outcome; (iv) the live registry is byte-identical afterwards. A mutation whose class cannot be tagged by construction fails the harness. Cites: SL-11, T-5, Tuning Knobs (`FUZZ_MUTATION_COUNT`).
14. **AC-SL-14 (auto) — block-level fuzz, never clamp.** GIVEN, per persisted block, 1 000 mutations (seed `BLOCK_FUZZ_SEED`) of its payload that pass the container checks, of which at least 100 are out-of-range value edits (a count below 0, a reference to a missing id, an enum outside its set), WHEN the block's `restore()` runs against a scratch registry, THEN each returns `(ok = false, reason, detail)` with `reason` in the block's declared closed `<BLOCK>_RESTORE_*` set, the live block is byte-identical after the failure, and no out-of-range value is accepted in a clamped form (a restore returning `ok = true` for an out-of-range edit fails). Save/Load supplies the harness; each owner supplies a fixture factory; a block with no fixture factory is a failing test (OQ-36). Cites: SL-15 items 5 and 6.
15. **AC-SL-15 (auto) — INCOMPATIBLE stops (parameterised).** GIVEN a campaign whose newest generation is re-signed to carry, one run per row: (1) `format_version` above ours; (2) `min_reader_version` above ours; (3) `tick_rate` ≠ `TICK_RATE`; (4) an unknown non-retired block id; (5) a stored `schema_version` outside the owner's `supported_versions`; (6) an unknown layout-id byte; (7) `codec` = 1; and two intact older generations, WHEN the loader runs, THEN the outcome is class INCOMPATIBLE with the reason of its row (`FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED`, `MIN_READER_EXCEEDED`, `TICK_RATE_MISMATCH`, `UNKNOWN_BLOCK`, `SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED`, `LAYOUT_UNKNOWN`, `FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED`), the loader stops, no older generation is loaded or modified, no file is moved to `quarantine/`, the kernel spy records zero calls, and the campaign state is `READ_ONLY`; AND WHEN the older build then reaches an autosave due-point, THEN it writes no generation into that campaign. The message string is the Chart Table's (provisional); this criterion asserts the reason code. Cites: SL-11, T-9.
16. **AC-SL-16 (auto) — CORRUPT falls back and quarantines.** GIVEN a ring whose newest file fails its trailer, WHEN the loader runs, THEN the next generation loads, the failed file is in `quarantine/`, `quarantine_reason(file)` (the host-local diagnostics read) returns `CORRUPT`, its bytes are unchanged, it is not deleted, and it is never a candidate afterwards. The physical encoding of the reason (suffix, sidecar or manifest entry) is an ADR choice and is not asserted (OQ-27). Cites: SL-11, SL-19.
17. **AC-SL-17 (auto) — retired, missing and added blocks.** GIVEN (a) a block on `RETIRED_BLOCKS`, (b) a missing non-mandatory block with `added_in_format` above the file's `format_version`, (c) a missing mandatory block, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN (a) is stripped with `SL_PRECHECK_RETIRED_BLOCK_STRIPPED`, (b) is filled by `default_state()` with a note, (c) is refused `SL_PRECHECK_MANDATORY_MISSING`; and an unknown non-retired id is INCOMPATIBLE, so the kernel's `RESTORE_UNKNOWN_BLOCK` is unreachable. Cites: SL-11, SL-14, T-5.
18. **AC-SL-18 (auto) — scope and perception blocks.** GIVEN a world file holding a PROFILE-scope block, a `profile.sav` holding a WORLD block, and a file holding a `PERCEPTION_OUT` block, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN each is refused (`SL_PRECHECK_SCOPE_MISMATCH` / `SL_PRECHECK_PERCEPTION_BLOCK_PRESENT`). Cites: SL-9, SL-24, T-5.
19. **AC-SL-19 (auto) — header cross-checks.** GIVEN re-signed files with: a header `T` unequal to the `kernel.clock` block; a header `world_seed` unequal to the block's; a missing or non-integer `catalogue_version`; a duplicate `block_id`; an offset gap; a length lie; a `seats` list that is empty or duplicated; a `PER_PLAYER` block holding a PlayerId key not in `seats`; and a `campaign_id` unequal to the directory name, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN each is refused with its named reason (`_HEADER_CLOCK_MISMATCH` for the first two, `_HEADER_FIELD_MISSING` / `_NOT_INT`, `_DUPLICATE_BLOCK_ID`, `_BLOCK_TABLE_INVALID`, `_PAYLOAD_LENGTH_MISMATCH`, `_SEATS_INVALID`, `_CAMPAIGN_MISMATCH`). Cites: SL-1, SL-20, T-5.
20. **AC-SL-20 (auto) — the pre-check is pure.** GIVEN the pre-check function, WHEN called twice with the same bytes, registry view and constants, THEN the results are identical and no clock, file handle or global is read. Cites: SL-11.
21. **AC-SL-21 (auto) — pre-check precedes the kernel.** GIVEN a file refused at any T-5 step, WHEN the loader returns, THEN the kernel control-plane spy saw no `request_restore` and the kernel never entered `FAULTED`. Cites: SL-11.
97. **AC-SL-97 (auto) — pre-check ordering.** GIVEN files each failing two steps (bad trailer plus unknown block; unknown block plus scope mismatch; missing mandatory plus bad seats; header clock mismatch plus duplicate block id), WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN the reason is that of the earlier step in T-5 order and exactly one reason is logged. Cites: T-5.
98. **AC-SL-98 (auto) — unreadable is transient.** GIVEN a candidate whose open fails (injected sharing violation) and an intact older generation, WHEN the loader runs, THEN `SL_PRECHECK_FILE_UNREADABLE` is logged, the loader falls back, the unreadable file is **not** moved to `quarantine/` and is a candidate again on the next load. Cites: T-5 step "open and read", SL-11.
99. **AC-SL-99 (auto) — structure steps on re-signed files.** GIVEN re-signed files with `header_len` = 0, = file size, and 0xFFFFFFFF; header bytes that are not JSON; a JSON array instead of an object; JSON nested deeper than `MAX_JSON_DEPTH` 8; and an unknown layout-id byte, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN the reasons are `HEADER_LEN_INVALID` (first three), `HEADER_NOT_JSON` (next three), and `LAYOUT_UNKNOWN` (INCOMPATIBLE, stop); and for the 0xFFFFFFFF case the allocation spy records no allocation above `MAX_HEADER_BYTES`. Cites: SL-1, T-5.
101. **AC-SL-101 (auto) — codec reserved.** GIVEN a re-signed header with `codec` = 1, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN `SL_PRECHECK_FORMAT_UNSUPPORTED`, class INCOMPATIBLE, stop (no decompression path exists). Cites: SL-19, T-5.
102. **AC-SL-102 (auto) — read-side caps.** GIVEN re-signed headers with a block length `MAX_BLOCK_BYTES + 1`, and with 129 block records, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN `SL_PRECHECK_BLOCK_TOO_LARGE` and `SL_PRECHECK_BLOCK_COUNT_EXCEEDS_MAX` respectively; 128 records is accepted. Cites: T-5, F-6.
103. **AC-SL-103 (auto) — block hash.** GIVEN a re-signed file with one `payload_sha256` altered, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN `SL_PRECHECK_BLOCK_HASH_MISMATCH` names that `block_id`, the class is CORRUPT, and the kernel spy records zero calls. Cites: SL-16, T-5.
104. **AC-SL-104 (auto) — migration chain completeness.** GIVEN a block with stored `schema_version` 1 whose owner lists {1, 2, 3} but registers only the 2 → 3 step, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN `SL_PRECHECK_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED` names the block and version 1; with the 1 → 2 step registered it passes. Cites: SL-14, T-5.
105. **AC-SL-105 (auto) — decode failure and clock block.** GIVEN a payload that is not a valid encoded value, a payload that decodes to an Array where a Dictionary is declared, a `kernel.clock` block with an extra key, a missing key, and a string `T`, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN `SL_PRECHECK_BLOCK_DECODE_FAILED` (first two) and `SL_PRECHECK_CLOCK_BLOCK_INVALID` (last three). Cites: T-5.
106. **AC-SL-106 (auto) — the mandatory set is derived.** GIVEN registrations with `added_in_format` of 1 and 2 and a format-1 file lacking the second, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN the second is filled by `default_state()` with a note; with `added_in_format` = 1 the file is refused `SL_PRECHECK_MANDATORY_MISSING`; no hand-kept list is read. Cites: SL-14.
107. **AC-SL-107 (auto) — seats.** GIVEN `seats` = [] / [1, 1] / [0] / [99999] (out of range), THEN each is refused `SL_PRECHECK_SEATS_INVALID`; GIVEN `seats` = [1, 2] with only PlayerId 1 bound, THEN the file is accepted (an unbound seat is allowed); GIVEN a `PER_PLAYER` key 3 with `seats` = [1, 2], THEN it is refused. Cites: SL-20, T-5.

### C. Generation ring, write procedure and crash recovery (SL-3, SL-10, T-3, T-4, T-6, F-9)

22. **AC-SL-22 (auto) — ring order is the header number.** GIVEN files whose modification times and `T` values disagree with their `generation` numbers (a step-back has rolled `T` back), WHEN the loader lists candidates, THEN they are ordered by header `generation` descending and `gen_next = 1 + max(generation over the ring, quarantine/, `.demoted` files and the manifest)`. Cites: SL-3, F-9.
23. **AC-SL-23 (auto) — first free position, newest untouched.** GIVEN the F-9 example ring (G7, demoted G6, G5, quarantined G9), WHEN the next write completes, THEN G10 is at `gen_1.sav`, the `.demoted` occupant was treated as an oldest occupant (renamed `.bak`, deleted after `POST_VERIFY`), and the newest generation's file was never renamed or touched at any step. Cites: SL-3, T-4 `SWAPPING_OUT`.
24. **AC-SL-24 (auto) — kill during write.** GIVEN a failing file-system adapter that aborts the process at each write state (`WRITING_TMP`, `VERIFYING`, before `SWAPPING_OUT`, between `SWAPPING_OUT` and `SWAPPING_IN`, between `SWAPPING_IN` and `POST_VERIFY`, `MANIFEST`) and at each of the same points of a `profile.sav` write, WHEN the campaign is reopened, THEN at least one loadable generation exists, no `.tmp` is a candidate, the recovery of T-6 yields the previous or the new generation, and a file that cannot be proven obsolete was not deleted; and for the kill point after `VERIFYING` and before `SWAPPING_OUT` both outcomes are exercised: a complete `.tmp` whose `generation` exceeds every ring generation is rolled forward, a complete `.tmp` whose `generation` does not is deleted (full tiebreak test: AC-SL-110). Cites: SL-10, T-6.
25. **AC-SL-25 (auto) — a failed write never harms.** GIVEN each injected fault — disk full, permission denied, open error, a `store_*` returning false, re-read hash mismatch, rename failure — applied at each write state in which the step performs that kind of operation (a matrix table in the test file lists the applicable cells; an inapplicable cell is marked, not skipped silently), WHEN the write runs, THEN every previous generation is byte-identical, `save_status` reads `FAILED`, `last_save_T` is unchanged, the `.tmp` is removed best-effort, and `SL_WRITE_FAILED{step}` is logged. GIVEN consecutive failures 1 through 6 at `AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS` 300 and `AUTOSAVE_FAIL_BACKOFF_MAX_TICKS` 4 800, THEN the retry delays are `min(AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS × 2^(n−1), AUTOSAVE_FAIL_BACKOFF_MAX_TICKS)` = 300, 600, 1 200, 2 400, 4 800, 4 800 ticks and the HUD-notice flag is raised on failure 3 (`AUTOSAVE_FAIL_ESCALATE_COUNT`) and not before (orchestrator ruling: the schedule is written into the knob row; OQ-26). Cites: SL-10, T-2, T-4, T-7.
26. **AC-SL-26 (auto) — every `store_*` return is checked.** GIVEN the writer, WHEN a `store_*` call is stubbed to return false at each call site in turn, THEN the write ends `FAILED` and never `WRITTEN`. Cites: SL-10 (sourced: `FileAccess.store_*` returns `bool` since 4.4).
27. **AC-SL-27 (auto) — epoch rule.** GIVEN a write job still running when a restore is issued, WHEN the epoch advances before the job's first rename, THEN the job aborts and removes its `.tmp`; and GIVEN a job already past `SWAPPING_IN`, THEN its generation is moved to `quarantine/` as `STALE_EPOCH` and is never a candidate. Cites: SL-10.
28. **AC-SL-28 (auto) — manifest is a hint.** GIVEN `campaign.json` deleted, corrupted, or disagreeing with the files, WHEN the campaign opens, THEN it is rebuilt from headers (`SL_MANIFEST_REBUILT`), the load is not refused, and demoted and quarantined generations stay excluded. Cites: SL-3.
29. **AC-SL-29 (auto) — write refuses what read would refuse.** GIVEN a block over `MAX_BLOCK_BYTES`, a file over `MAX_SAVE_BYTES` and a header over `MAX_HEADER_BYTES`, WHEN a save is attempted, THEN `SL_WRITE_TOO_LARGE{block}` is reported, no file is produced and the ring is untouched. Cites: F-6.
30. **AC-SL-30 (spike) — Windows rename and flush.** GIVEN each machine configuration (SSD, HDD, antivirus on, OneDrive-redirected `user://`), WHEN the write procedure runs 1 000 times per configuration with sharing violations injected at the rename step on 10 % of runs, THEN after every run (including runs killed at the injection point) at least one loadable generation exists, the number of runs that leave zero loadable generations is 0, no run depends on rename-onto-existing, and the measured `flush` → fsync behaviour and the 99th-percentile `durable_ms` per configuration are recorded in `production/qa/evidence/spike-save-windows.md`. ### D. Autosave schedule and gate (SL-4, SL-5, F-1, F-2)

31. **AC-SL-31 (auto) — interval trigger.** GIVEN `AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_TICKS` 10 800, `last_save_T = 0` and an open gate, WHEN `T` reaches 10 800, THEN exactly one `fire` occurs with kind `AUTO` and `risky = false`. Cites: F-1, F-2.

Cites: F-1, SL-4.
33. **AC-SL-33 (auto) — no interval save while paused.** GIVEN the kernel `PAUSED` for 20 000 ticks of wall time, WHEN the scheduler runs, THEN no interval save becomes due. Cites: SL-4, T-1.
34. **AC-SL-34 (auto) — autosave is suppressed while `DOWNED`.** GIVEN a seat in `DOWNED` when an interval save falls due at `T = 10 800`, WHEN the gate is evaluated at every retry tick (300-tick grid), THEN no snapshot is requested, including at the `AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS` cap tick (`T = 18 000`); and once the seat is revived the next retry tick writes the save, not `RISKY`. Cites: SL-5, F-2.
35. **AC-SL-35 (auto) — soft deferral, the `RISKY` cap and the tell mode.** GIVEN a continuous soft block (injected Attention view or Graph fixture) from `due_first_T = 10 800` and no hard block, WHEN `waited` reaches 7 200 on the retry grid, THEN the save fires at `T = 18 000` with `risky = true`. With `gate_tell_mode = FAIR`: an Attention tell with 601 ticks remaining does not block; exactly 600 remaining does not block; 599 remaining blocks (`tell_remaining < W_MIN_WARNING_S × TICK_RATE`). With `STRICT`: an Attention tell with 700 ticks remaining at 10 800 blocks at 10 800, 11 100 and 11 400, and the save fires at 11 700 after the tell ends. Each of `CHANNELLING`, `STAGGERED`, `IN_POD` in `GATE_SOFT_STATES` blocks softly (`RISKY` if the cap elapsed); `IDLE`, `WALK`, `RUN`, `CARRY_WALK` do not; a `breach_phase` other than `CALM` blocks softly; `gate_tell_mode` has no effect on a Graph descriptor (AC-SL-125). Cites: SL-5, F-2.
36. **AC-SL-36 (auto) — absent provisional view.** GIVEN no Attention read view published (the Graph view present), WHEN the gate runs, THEN `GATE_INPUT_ABSENT` is logged once per process lifetime (a death-stub restore inside the same process does not log it again) and the Graph, controller and kernel conditions alone decide; the Graph half (AC-SL-125) is unaffected and never logs `GATE_INPUT_ABSENT` *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)* (orchestrator ruling: "session" = process lifetime; OQ-29). Cites: SL-5.
37. **AC-SL-37 (auto) — no generation ever holds a `DOWNED` or `DEAD` seat.** GIVEN a scripted session of 10 000 ticks generated from `INVARIANT_FUZZ_SEED` with injected `DOWNED`, revive and `DEAD` events and autosaves, quits and window-closes at scripted ticks, repeated for 5 distinct seeds, WHEN every generation file written is decoded, THEN none contains a seat whose `controller.state.loco_state` is `DOWNED` or `DEAD`. I9: `AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS` raised so `loss_max_stepback` = 23 401 logs a diagnostic only and boot continues; the default 23 400 (equality) logs nothing. Knobs outside their safe range but violating no invariant are not covered here (Gaps; OQ-28). Cites: Tuning Knobs invariants.
39. **AC-SL-39 (auto) — timers reconstruct.** GIVEN a process restart that restores a generation with `T = 54 000`, WHEN the scheduler starts, THEN `last_save_T = 54 000`, no autosave is immediately due, and the first interval save is due at 64 800. Cites: SL-3, T-2.

An event type not in the set never schedules a save. Cites: SL-4, F-1.

### E. Save & Quit, window close (SL-6)

41. **AC-SL-41 (auto) — Save & Quit writes a `QUIT` generation then shuts down.** GIVEN the kernel `PAUSED` and all seats alive, WHEN `save_and_quit()` is called, THEN the kernel goes `PAUSED → SAVING → PAUSED` with `T` unchanged, a generation of kind `QUIT` is `WRITTEN`, and only then is `request_shutdown` issued. Cites: SL-6.
42. **AC-SL-42 (auto) — Save & Quit with a seat `DOWNED` writes nothing.** GIVEN any bound seat `DOWNED`, WHEN `save_and_quit()` is called, THEN it returns `SL_QUIT_NO_WRITE_DOWNED`, no file is written, `save_status` is unchanged, the shutdown proceeds only after the hold-to-confirm "quit without saving", and Continue afterwards restores the newest verified generation. Cites: SL-6, T-2.
43. **AC-SL-43 (auto) — Save & Quit with only a soft condition closed.** GIVEN a seat `CHANNELLING` or an injected non-calm breach phase, WHEN `save_and_quit()` is called, THEN the generation is written immediately with `risky = true`. Cites: SL-6, F-2.
44. **AC-SL-44 (auto) — window close from `RUNNING`.** GIVEN the kernel `RUNNING`, WHEN the window-close request arrives, THEN `save_and_quit()` runs `RUNNING → SAVING → RUNNING` and `request_shutdown` follows the `WRITTEN`; with a seat `DOWNED` nothing is written. GIVEN the kernel `PAUSED` under the open Chart Table, WHEN the window-close request arrives, THEN `save_and_quit()` runs `PAUSED → SAVING → PAUSED`, `request_shutdown` follows and the Chart Table calls nothing; GIVEN a boot-flow screen, THEN nothing is written *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass)*. Cites: SL-6.
45a. **AC-SL-45a (auto) — quit dedupe.** GIVEN a state hash equal to the newest generation's, WHEN `save_and_quit()` is called, THEN the write is skipped (`SL_QUIT_DEDUPED`), no file is written and shutdown proceeds. Cites: SL-6.
45b. **AC-SL-45b (auto) — quit with a failed write.** GIVEN an injected write failure, WHEN `save_and_quit()` is called, THEN the quit is held, `save_status` reads `FAILED`, the pause-panel flag is set, and a retry is possible. Cites: SL-6.
45c. **AC-SL-45c (auto) — quit with a slow write.** GIVEN a write exceeding `SAVE_QUIT_TIMEOUT_MS` (5 000 ms of injected time), WHEN `save_and_quit()` is called, THEN at 5 000 ms the quit is held, `save_status` reads `FAILED`, and the pause-panel flag is set. Cites: SL-6.
115. **AC-SL-115 (auto) — same-tick collisions.** GIVEN (a) Save & Quit and an interval save due on the same tick: one `QUIT` generation, no `AUTO` generation, `due_first_T` discarded; (b) an autosave due and `DEAD` entered at the same boundary: no `request_snapshot`, the restore proceeds; (c) `request_restore` arriving while the kernel is `SAVING`: the capture completes, the stub waits for `WRITTEN` or `FAILED`, then selects among verified candidates including the new one. Cites: SL-6, SL-7, F-2, kernel `SAVING` rule.

### F. Death stub, selection and demotion (SL-7, SL-8, T-8, F-3, F-7)

46. **AC-SL-46 (auto) — the stub restores the newest verified generation.** GIVEN a ring of three verified generations and a seat reaching `DEAD`, WHEN the tick boundary runs, THEN in that boundary the stub waits for any in-flight write, pre-checks and stages the newest non-demoted generation, and only then issues `request_restore(payload)`; the kernel enters `LOADING`, the command queue is flushed `Dropped(RELOAD)`, and the first `RUNNING` tick sees an empty queue. Cites: SL-7, T-8.
47. **AC-SL-47 (auto) — nothing is read before the pre-check passes.** GIVEN the newest generation refused by the pre-check, WHEN the stub runs, THEN it falls back to the next candidate before `request_restore`, and the kernel is touched at most once. Cites: SL-7, SL-11.
48. **AC-SL-48 (auto) — no loadable save.** GIVEN every candidate refused, WHEN the stub runs, THEN `SL_LOAD_NO_LOADABLE_SAVE` is published, the kernel stays untouched, Save/Load issues no pause, no file is deleted, and no autosave of the dead world occurs. Cites: SL-7.
49a. **AC-SL-49a (auto) — the stub waits for an in-flight write.** GIVEN a write in flight when `DEAD` is entered, WHEN the stub runs, THEN it waits for `WRITTEN` or `FAILED` before bumping the epoch and selecting, so the write is not aborted by its own bump; the written generation is a candidate for the selection that follows. Cites: SL-7, SL-10, T-8.
49b. **AC-SL-49b (auto) — the initial baseline failed.** GIVEN the initial `NEW_GAME` write `FAILED` when `DEAD` is entered, WHEN the stub runs, THEN `SL_NEW_GAME_NO_BASELINE` is raised, the empty campaign directory is removed and no `request_restore` is issued. Cites: SL-18, SL-7.
50. **AC-SL-50 (auto) — `RISKY` step-back and demotion.** GIVEN ring G7 (`T = 40 000`, `RISKY`), G6 (29 000), G5 (18 000), G7 restored, and `T_downed = 41 200`, `DEAD` at 43 000, WHEN the stub selects, THEN G6 is chosen and G7 is renamed `.demoted` and absent from the candidate list even after `campaign.json` is deleted; a second death before any autosave restores G6 again, never G7. Cites: SL-8, F-3.
51. **AC-SL-51 (auto) — no step-back for a non-`RISKY` generation.** GIVEN the same ring with G7 untagged, WHEN the crew dies at `T_downed` = `g.T`, `g.T + 1`, `g.T + 1 200`, `g.T + 3 600`, `g.T + 3 601` and `g.T + 10 000` after the restore, THEN G7 is restored in all six. Cites: SL-8, F-3, OQ-1.
52. **AC-SL-52 (auto) — the window is measured at `DOWNED`.** GIVEN a `RISKY` g_r with `T_downed − g_r.T = 3 600` (inside) and 3 601 (outside), and `DOWN_TO_DEATH_TICKS` varied across 600, 1 800 and 3 600, WHEN the stub selects, THEN the inside case steps back and the outside case does not, independently of `DOWN_TO_DEATH_TICKS`. Cites: SL-8, F-3.
53. **AC-SL-53 (auto) — no loop when a newer generation exists.** GIVEN a `RISKY` g_r and a newer non-demoted verified generation written after the restore, WHEN the crew dies within the window, THEN the newer generation is restored and nothing is demoted. Cites: SL-8 (b).
54. **AC-SL-54 (auto) — degenerate rings.** GIVEN (a) one candidate that is `RISKY` with a loop, (b) only demoted candidates, (c) no restored generation yet, THEN (a) restores it and logs `SL_STEPBACK_NONE_AVAILABLE`, (b) a second pass restores the newest demoted one, (c) the newest is restored with no loop test. Cites: F-3.
55. **AC-SL-55 (auto) — loss bounds.** GIVEN 10 000 scripted death times generated from `LOSS_FUZZ_SEED` over the scenarios (clean cycle, continuous soft block, `DOWNED` at the cap, one conditional step-back) at the default knobs with `EVENT_TRIGGERS` = {`NEW_GAME`} only (events can only lower the bounds), WHEN `loss_ticks` is computed, THEN `min ≥ 1 801`, `max ≤ 19 800` without a step-back and `≤ 23 400` with one, and the mean over uniform death phase with an open gate is within 1 % of 7 200. Cites: F-7.
56. **AC-SL-56 (auto) — the stub keeps `k_z` and attaches no penalty.** GIVEN a death stub run, WHEN the first `RUNNING` frame is produced, THEN the Pipeline test double reports the pre-load `k_z`, a `CUT`, and no death-screen, lights-out or beat state; no item, stat or counter other than the restored blocks differs from the generation. Cites: SL-7, SL-21.
57a. **AC-SL-57a (auto) — owner failure in `LOADING` is `FAULTED`, not retried in line.** GIVEN an owner `restore()` returning a `<BLOCK>_RESTORE_*` failure on the staged payload, WHEN `LOADING` runs, THEN the kernel enters `FAULTED`, the loader does not retry, and the menu's Continue re-runs the pipeline on the remaining candidates with `restore_reason = FAULT_RECOVERY`. Cites: SL-11, SL-22.
57b. **AC-SL-57b (auto) — failure counter and quarantine.** GIVEN the same generation failing across Continue runs, THEN its failure counter increments per failure and persists across the runs; at `QUARANTINE_AFTER_FAILURES` (2) it moves to `quarantine/` with reason `RESTORE_FAILED` and the next Continue loads the next candidate. 57c. **AC-SL-57c (auto) — dev-build `FAULTED`.** GIVEN a dev build, WHEN the kernel enters `FAULTED`, THEN the build halts with diagnostics, offers no menu path, and makes no automatic revert from the in-memory pre-restore snapshot. Cites: SL-22.
112. **AC-SL-112 (auto) — no `DOWNED` observed.** GIVEN `DEAD` entered with no prior `DOWNED` observation, WHEN `generation_select` runs, THEN `T_downed = T_dead`, the hard condition has closed the gate at that boundary, and selection follows F-3 with that value. Cites: F-3.
113. **AC-SL-113 (auto) — re-entrancy and attempt bound.** GIVEN `continue_campaign()` called twice while the kernel is `LOADING`, THEN the second returns `SL_LOAD_REENTRANT`, with one epoch bump and one restore. GIVEN a ring of 5 (`AUTOSAVE_GENERATIONS` 5) with `LOAD_MAX_ATTEMPTS` 3 and the 3 newest corrupt, THEN exactly 3 pre-check attempts run and `SL_LOAD_NO_LOADABLE_SAVE` is published even though the fourth is intact; with `LOAD_MAX_ATTEMPTS` 5 the fourth loads. 119b. **AC-SL-119b (review) — a re-signed hand edit loads (accepted exposure).** GIVEN a hand-edited file re-signed with a valid trailer and valid block hashes whose payload decodes validly, WHEN the pre-check runs, THEN it loads; this section records the case as an accepted exposure and no criterion requires a tamper rejection (integrity is against accidental corruption, not tampering — host-owned, no PvP, no leaderboard). Cites: SL-16.

### H. PROFILE store, identity and what is not saved (SL-9, SL-17, SL-20, SL-24, SL-25)

69. **AC-SL-69 (auto) — PROFILE untouched by a world restore.** GIVEN a synthetic PROFILE block mutated after the snapshot, WHEN the death stub restores a generation, THEN the WORLD blocks roll back, the PROFILE block still holds every post-snapshot mutation, and `request_restore` was passed WORLD blocks only; and WHEN the process is restarted and Continue is used, THEN each scope loads whole from its own file. Cites: SL-9, kernel CR-18 (its PROFILE-untouched criterion is the model).
70. **AC-SL-70 (auto) — `profile.sav` rules.** GIVEN (a) zero PROFILE blocks registered and no `profile.sav`, (b) one registered and no `profile.sav` and no `.bak`, (c) a `.bak` only, (d) a profile write killed mid-way, THEN (a) opens with an empty profile, (b) refuses `SL_PRECHECK_MANDATORY_MISSING` for the profile, the world still loads and the profile resets with `SL_PROFILE_RESET`, (c) the `.bak` is used, (d) T-6 recovers and the ring is unaffected; and the file is rewritten only when a capture's PROFILE per-block hashes differ, on `QUIT`, or on an owner notification, never faster than `PROFILE_MIN_WRITE_GAP_TICKS`. Cites: SL-9.
71. **AC-SL-71 (auto) — zero blocks, zero hooks.** GIVEN Save/Load booted, WHEN the registry is inspected, THEN the only registered block is `kernel.clock` plus the test blocks, no hook or command type belongs to Save/Load (`saveload.request_save` is reserved, provisional, and not registered), and any attempt by Save/Load to register fails `SL_BOOT_SAVELOAD_REGISTERS_BLOCK`. Cites: SL-17, the kernel's single-block criterion.
72. **AC-SL-72 (auto) — `PERCEPTION_OUT` is absent from every file.** GIVEN a session with active sanity and a perception-output block registered, WHEN a generation and `profile.sav` are written, THEN neither file contains the perception block or any field describing what the player saw. Cites: SL-24, CR-12.
73. **AC-SL-73 (auto) — identity is the seat.** GIVEN a peer rebinding to a different transport id, WHEN the next generation is written and the previous one loaded, THEN every `PER_PLAYER` key is the same PlayerId, no file contains a peer id, and the binding table is absent from the file. Cites: SL-20, kernel CR-7.
74. **AC-SL-74 (auto) — settings and camera are not in a snapshot.** GIVEN a session with changed key bindings and a non-default zoom, WHEN a generation is written, THEN no settings, binding, camera or zoom field appears in any block. Cites: SL-19, SL-25.
96b. **AC-SL-96b (auto) — scope matrix.** GIVEN a synthetic block for each of `WORLD × SHARED`, `WORLD × PER_PLAYER`, `PROFILE × SHARED`, `PROFILE × PER_PLAYER`, each mutated after the snapshot, WHEN the death stub restores, THEN the two WORLD blocks roll back to their generation values and the two PROFILE blocks keep every post-snapshot mutation; `PER_PLAYER` data is keyed by PlayerId in both. `suit.state`, `sanity.state` and `sanity.ledger` are `PER_PLAYER × WORLD` in the MVP and must roll back (shapes per the designed Suit and Sanity GDDs). Cites: SL-9, SL-15 item 12.
116. **AC-SL-116 (auto) — PROFILE flush before restore.** GIVEN a PROFILE write pending when the death stub fires, THEN the profile is flushed first, `request_restore` receives WORLD blocks only, and no byte of `profile.sav` reaches a WORLD block. Cites: SL-9, SL-7.
123. **AC-SL-123 (auto) — the VS-A replacement keeps the PROFILE rule.** GIVEN a test double standing in for Failure Contract & Re-wake (provisional — undesigned) that restores a generation by the same `request_restore` path with a PROFILE block mutated, WHEN it runs in place of the stub, THEN the PROFILE block is kept and the WORLD blocks roll back (AC-SL-69 with the stub replaced), so the replacement cannot silently regress SL-9. Cites: SL-7, SL-9.

### I. New game, directory and API (SL-18, SL-19, SL-21, T-9)

75. **AC-SL-75 (auto) — new game baseline.** GIVEN `new_game(world_seed)` with a stand-in world builder and an injected entropy source supplying a fixed 16-byte value, WHEN the world is constructed, THEN the campaign directory exists and its 32-character lowercase-hex name equals the entropy value, `world_seed` is in `kernel.clock`, an initial generation (kind `EVENT`, trigger `NEW_GAME`) is `WRITTEN` at the first boundary after construction, and on construction failure or `AUTOSAVE_FAIL_ESCALATE_COUNT` baseline failures the empty directory is removed and `SL_NEW_GAME_NO_BASELINE` is raised; a second `new_game` with the same entropy value never overwrites the existing directory and re-draws (orchestrator ruling: OQ-31). Cites: SL-18, T-9.
76. **AC-SL-76 (auto) — host-local API surface.** GIVEN the release API, WHEN introspected, THEN it exposes `new_game`, `continue_campaign`, `save_and_quit`, `campaign_info`, `list_saves`, `retire_campaign` and `delete_campaign`, and exposes no `save_now` and no `load(generation)` (a dev build may). Cites: SL-4, SL-21.
77. **AC-SL-77 (auto) — retire before delete.** GIVEN New campaign over an existing campaign, WHEN confirmed, THEN the old campaign no longer appears in `list_saves()`, its files are byte-identical at their new location, and `continue_campaign()` opens the new one; deletion occurs only through the separate `delete_campaign` action. The retire path is an ADR choice and is asserted through the API only (OQ-27). Cites: SL-19.
78. **AC-SL-78 (auto) — quarantine is never auto-deleted.** GIVEN a quarantine folder beyond `SL_QUARANTINE_SIZE_ALARM_BYTES`, WHEN a long session of 100 writes and 10 failures runs, THEN a diagnostics alarm is logged and no quarantined file is deleted. Cites: SL-19, Tuning Knobs.
79. **AC-SL-79 (auto) — `FAULTED` never writes.** GIVEN the kernel in `FAULTED`, WHEN an autosave comes due, a Save & Quit is requested and a snapshot is requested, THEN no file in the campaign directory is created, renamed or modified, and the kernel hands no payload. Cites: SL-22, the kernel's `FAULTED` rule.
80. **AC-SL-80 (auto) — `restore_reason` on the first `RUNNING` frame.** GIVEN each path (`NEW_GAME`, `LOAD_GAME`, `DEATH_STUB`, `FAULT_RECOVERY`), WHEN `LOADING → RUNNING` occurs, THEN the host-local read reports the matching value for the first `RUNNING` frame, and no consumer in the MVP alters hallucination content from `DEATH_STUB`. Cites: SL-21, OQ-8.
81. **AC-SL-81 (auto) — `save_status` and `SaveInfo`.** GIVEN a write, WHEN it progresses, THEN `save_status` reads `IDLE → WRITING → WRITTEN` (sticky, with `written_at_T`) or `FAILED`, and `SaveInfo` (campaign id, generation, kind, `T`, playtime, `game_version`, crew count, `RISKY`, depth label) is computed from SIM blocks at capture, contains no thumbnail field, and its depth label (provisional — derived from the node `ring` of `web.view`) equals the header value; playtime is `T div TICK_RATE` whole seconds (at `T = 437 400` it is 7 290 s; orchestrator ruling: integer floor, OQ-30); `fail_escalated` is false through two consecutive failed writes, true from the third (`AUTOSAVE_FAIL_ESCALATE_COUNT`) and false again after the next `WRITTEN`; and `campaign_info().newest_failed_verification` is true for a ring whose newest generation has a corrupted trailer and false otherwise *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass)*. Cites: SL-21, T-7.
96e. **AC-SL-96e (auto) — Item DB header fields.** GIVEN `catalogue_version` = 7 and a `catalogue_hash`, WHEN a generation is written and read, THEN both round-trip; a changed `catalogue_hash` alone never refuses a load; an unknown `def_id` at restore surfaces as the owner's named failure (for example `GRAPH_RESTORE_UNKNOWN_DEF`) and is never a Save/Load refusal; GIVEN `catalogue_version` = 9 in the header and 7 in the running catalogue, THEN the pre-check does not refuse and the holder's restore decides. Cites: SL-1, SL-14.

### J. Cross-system criteria

82. **AC-SL-82 (auto) — Graph through the container.** GIVEN the real `graph.state` at the MVP slice size, WHEN written and restored through disk, THEN the restore order places the `layout.state` stand-in before `graph.state` (kernel order, never Save/Load's), the encoded `graph.state` payload is ≤ 32 KB, restore plus `post_restore` reports success (`GRAPH_RESTORE_*` is not raised), and no packed array travels as a JSON number list. Cites: SL-2, SL-25, S-1, Graph LS-21.
82b. **AC-SL-82b (spike) — Graph cost.** GIVEN the same fixture on the target machine, WHEN run 1 000 times, THEN the snapshot costs ≤ 0.5 ms and restore plus `post_restore` ≤ 2 ms at P99 (the Graph's own criterion re-run through the container). Cites: Graph LS-21, F-5.
83. **AC-SL-83 (auto) — Controller.** GIVEN a Controller test double, WHEN a seat enters `DEAD`, THEN Save/Load issues `request_restore` in that tick's boundary, the Presenter holds the down sheet, no second camera cut is issued, and `DEAD` during a camera cut is handled. Cites: SL-7, the Controller's `DEAD` row.
84. **AC-SL-84 (manual) — Pipeline.** GIVEN an in-engine run, WHEN `LOADING` is entered mid-session, THEN `TRANSIT_HOLD` shows layer-1 black, zoom presses are discarded, and the first `RUNNING` frame is a camera `CUT` at the pre-load `k_z`; retained screenshots of the black frame and of the first `RUNNING` frame are in `production/qa/evidence/`. Cites: SL-7, Pipeline `TRANSIT_HOLD` and C5.
84b. **AC-SL-84b (review) — the routed Pipeline text fix exists.** GIVEN the Open Questions section, WHEN reviewed, THEN OQ-14 carries the row saying the Pipeline's 2.10 traceability entry is built in Failure Contract & Re-wake, not in the stub (this criterion checks the row, not another GDD's text). Cites: OQ-14.
85. **AC-SL-85 (review) — routed amendments are carried as routed.** GIVEN the Open Questions section, WHEN reviewed, THEN K-1, the D.10 note, K-2, K-3, K-4, the observation channel, `restore_reason`, the "no clean frame" ordering, the stale-token list and the index label clarification are rows OQ-2 … OQ-15 addressed to their owners, and none is written as an existing rule of another GDD. Cites: Open Questions.
86. **AC-SL-86 (lint + review) — stale tokens.** GIVEN the GDD, WHEN searched for "reload last save", "keyed by peer ID", "single-slot autosave", "P5 snapshot", "respawn", "save point", "game over", "you died", "progress saved", "quicksave", THEN every hit is on a line allow-listed in `tests/lint/save-load-stale-token-allowlist.txt` (negations and the stale-token row) and a reviewer signs the allow-list; AND GIVEN every player-facing string authored for Save/Load (pause panel, load screen, notices), THEN none contains a stale token, an exclamation mark, or a second-person death phrase. Cites: Player Fantasy fixed terms, OQ-14.
95a. **AC-SL-95a (auto) — the observation channel is exactly the granted reads.** GIVEN Save/Load booted with a kernel spy and a registry spy, WHEN a full session runs (autosaves, a death stub, a Save & Quit), THEN its recorded reads are exactly `kernel.clock`, `controller.state.loco_state` per bound seat, the Graph's `read_view.failures()`, the optional Attention SIM view `attention.view`, the event-trigger reads of SL-4 (`POD_ARRIVED`, `last_shape_change_T()`, Interaction's `last_salvage_T()`), and the end-of-tick change notifications; no `PERCEPTION_OUT` view is read; wall clock is never read inside a decision (only `created_unix` metadata is written). Cites: SL-13, SL-23, SL-24, CR-12.
95b. **AC-SL-95b (lint) — Save/Load never writes the sim.** GIVEN the Save/Load source, WHEN searched, THEN no call to a block mutator or `restore()` appears; the worker module imports no kernel and no block module; `created_unix` is written and never read by a decision. Recommended gate level BLOCKING (guards the kernel-write boundary). Cites: SL-10, SL-13, SL-17.
121. **AC-SL-121 (review) — Attention soft-gate view (blocked).** GIVEN the designed Attention GDD, WHEN reviewed, THEN its SIM read view `attention.view` carries `tell_active`, `tell_remaining` (0 … `TELL_MAX_TICKS`) and `breach_phase`, and a view returning a `PERCEPTION_OUT`-derived value fails AC-SL-95a. Unblocked 2026-10-27 (consistency pass); the Graph half of the tell condition is not blocked (AC-SL-125) *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)*. Cites: SL-5, SL-13, OQ-24.
122. **AC-SL-122 (review) — Emitter and Body restore order (blocked).** GIVEN `emitter.state` with `restore_after` [`layout.state`, `graph.state`, `body.state`, `controller.state`] (all four designed: Layout LY-1, Body BDY-5, Emitter EM-1), WHEN restored through the container with stand-ins, THEN the kernel's order is honoured, and with `body.state` unregistered boot fails `SL_BOOT_RESTORE_AFTER_UNREGISTERED`. Unblocked: `body.state` is registered by Ship Body & Interior Physics (BDY-5; OQ-10 closed). Cites: SL-25, OQ-10.
124. **AC-SL-124 (review) — the "no clean frame" ordering stays recorded.** GIVEN the Perception Filter, Sanity or Pipeline GDD when it lands, WHEN reviewed, THEN OQ-11 is either answered with an owner or still open with no owner, and AC-SL-93 is re-run. Cites: SL-24, OQ-11.
127. **AC-SL-127 (auto) — the Overlay diagnostics channel** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from debug-overlay-true-values)*. GIVEN a dev-tag build with a channel-registry spy, WHEN the composition root runs, THEN Save/Load registers exactly one channel, `save_load`, while the kernel is `BOOTING`, with the eight rows of the Debug Overlay row in Interactions; WHEN a write fails, THEN `pull()` reports `save_status` `FAILED` and the last `SL_*` reason; WHEN `pull()` is called during a full session, THEN Save/Load's recorded kernel and block reads are unchanged from AC-SL-95a; GIVEN a release-tag build, THEN no channel is registered. Cites: debug-overlay-true-values DO-2, DO-12, AC-DO-69; registry `diag_panel_contract`.

### K. Performance, feel and presentation

87. **AC-SL-87 (spike) — capture cost unchanged.** GIVEN the MVP slice (8 blocks, about 61 KiB) on the target machine, WHEN 1 000 autosaves run, THEN `main_hitch_ms` (= `capture_time_ms + t_encode + t_handoff`) is ≤ `SNAPSHOT_BUDGET_MS` (16.7 ms) at P99; `t_encode` ≤ `SAVE_ENCODE_BUDGET_MS`; `t_handoff` ≤ `WRITE_HANDOFF_MAX_FRACTION × SNAPSHOT_BUDGET_MS`; `durable_ms` ≤ `WRITE_DURABLE_ALARM_MS`; `worker_ms` ≤ 30 000 ms (`DOWN_TO_DEATH_TICKS × 1000 / TICK_RATE`) at the maximum observed; every exceeded threshold is logged and no write is truncated; and no frame between `WRITING_TMP` and `MANIFEST` exceeds 25 ms. The measured encoding-overhead ratio and the header-size constants (`H0` 500 – 700, `h_b` 100 – 180) are recorded. Cites: F-5, F-6 (orchestrator ruling: the one-frame target applies to the main-thread sum; OQ-33).
88. **AC-SL-88 (spike) — restart time.** GIVEN the death stub on the target machine with an MVP-size generation, WHEN `DEAD` to first `RUNNING` frame is timed over 1 000 runs, THEN `restart_ms` ≤ `LOADING_TARGET_MS` at P99 and the `DEAD`-tick hitch from the pre-check is recorded; if missed, the alternatives of OQ-23 are evaluated. Cites: F-8, SL-11.
89. **AC-SL-89 (spike) — hash and decode cost.** GIVEN the chosen 64-bit hash (xxHash64 or FNV-1a in GDScript) over 100 – 300 KiB, WHEN measured on capture and on restore, THEN `t_hash_capture` and `t_hash_restore` are recorded to `production/qa/evidence/spike-hash.md` and the K-1 / hash-ADR decision (OQ-2, OQ-17) is written beside the number; pass = a decision exists for the measured value, and if `t_hash_capture` exceeds the kernel's 1.5 ms assumption the decision names the mitigation (per-block cache or a native hash). Cites: SL-16.
90. **AC-SL-90 (spike) — engine claims.** GIVEN the spike, WHEN run, THEN a table records, per claim, `confirmed` or `amended` (with the amended rule id): `var_to_bytes` int-exactness and signed-`u64` behaviour; typed-container and `StringName`-key encoding; duplicate-key JSON behaviour; native SHA-256 throughput; the worker hand-off and thread file access; rename and flush semantics; and peak memory and time to decode a 32 MiB hostile payload (nested arrays, array of nulls, large integer-keyed dictionary) against the ceiling set in OQ-35; the count of [UNV] tags left in the GDD equals the count of still-open spike items (lint). Cites: OQ-16, OQ-35.
91. **AC-SL-91 (manual) — the save is imperceptible.** GIVEN a 30-minute playtest at 60 Hz with a frame-time log, autosaves, at least one event save and exactly one death, WHEN reviewed, THEN (a) no frame in the log exceeds 25 ms within ±2 frames of a capture start; (b) no sound, text or mark announces an autosave anywhere on the HUD (the failed-write notice stays absent while writes succeed; checked by capture review, not recollection) *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)*; (c) the stopwatch-measured black hold from the `DEAD` tick to the first `RUNNING` frame is ≥ 120 ms (the `TRANSIT_HOLD` fade) and `restart_ms` ≤ `LOADING_TARGET_MS`, both values written on the evidence sheet; (d) a screenshot of the first `RUNNING` frame after the death is retained in `production/qa/evidence/` with lead sign-off. Cites: Game Feel, SL-21.
92. **AC-SL-92 (manual + lint) — presentation guard rails.** GIVEN each screen touched by Save/Load (pause panel with its newest-record line, hold-to-confirm "quit without saving", load screen, `SL_LOAD_NO_LOADABLE_SAVE`, INCOMPATIBLE message, `FAILED` notice), WHEN built, THEN a screenshot of each is retained in `production/qa/evidence/` and shows no thumbnail, no ring or percentage, no death screen; the `FAILED` notice's and the newest-record line's sampled pixel colours are not within the art bible's Flesh (`#D9A58C`, `#B5705C`) or Violet (`#8A3FD1`, `#B07BE6`) entries (sampled RGB and the four hex codes written on the sheet); destructive actions are hold-to-confirm on a linear bar (hold duration in ms written on the sheet and verified by stopwatch; the value is the UX spec's, OQ-32); and a capture sequence across at least 5 autosaves, taken with the gate deferring (an injected tell) and with it open, shows HUD pixels identical outside the notice region, so no HUD element differs between a write, a deferral and neither *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)*. GIVEN the Save/Load source, WHEN linted, THEN it calls no audio-playback API and no viewport-capture API; GIVEN the shipped strings, THEN they pass the AC-SL-86 token lint. Cites: SL-21, SL-24, UI Requirements, art bible 7.4 and HR-14.
93. **AC-SL-93 (manual) — hallucination interplay.** GIVEN a playtest session with a false system state active, WHEN the crew dies and the stub restores, THEN every persisted counter and seed read after the restore (the owners' `rng_derive` counters, via the debug overlay or the stand-in log) equals the generation's, the sanity level read after the restore equals the generation's, and a screenshot of the first `RUNNING` frame (grabbed by the frame-capture tool, not from memory) is retained and shows the filter applied for that sanity level. The criterion deliberately does **not** assert that the false state's onset tick is identical or different between runs: that is Sanity's decision (OQ-37) *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)*. If the first frame is unfiltered, OQ-11 is raised and this criterion is recorded FAILED, not deferred. Cites: SL-23, SL-24, H2 – H4.
94. **AC-SL-94 (manual) — kill / Save & Quit / die / Continue playtest.** GIVEN the first build given outside the project and three testers, WHEN each kills the process, Save & Quits, dies and chooses Continue, THEN every one of those campaigns loads (12 of 12, or all attempted if fewer actions were completed), and each tester completes questionnaire items Q1 – Q4 (is the cost of dying fair, 1 – 5; is quit-and-relaunch a cheat, 1 – 5; did anything lose your progress, y/n; did you notice an autosave, y/n). In addition, the playtest build logs and the sheet records, per tester, the three triggers of Edge Cases — **M1** `deaths_per_restored_gen`, **M2** `scout_death_rate` (`T_dead − T_enter` ≤ `SCOUT_DEATH_WINDOW_TICKS` 5 400, `T_enter` = Web Map & Transit's derelict-level `FIRST_BOARDED{derelict_id, T}` at the MVP, OQ-25) and **M3** `quit_relaunch_rate` (Save & Quit then Continue within `QUIT_RELAUNCH_WINDOW_S` 120 s) — each against its starting threshold and, where tripped, the named response (retune `AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_TICKS` once, otherwise escalate to Failure Contract & Re-wake at VS-A). A tripped trigger with no recorded response is an incomplete sheet. The fairness numbers and the questionnaire stay ADVISORY: a tripped trigger opens OQ-19 or OQ-25 work, it does not fail the build; "campaigns load" stays BLOCKING *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)*. Cites: SL-14, SL-23.
117. **AC-SL-117 (manual) — UI evidence set.** GIVEN the screens listed in UI Requirements, WHEN built, THEN the screenshot set of AC-SL-92 exists in `production/qa/evidence/` with one image per screen and the checks on the sheet pass. This is the retained-screenshot evidence the UI row of the testing standard requires. Cites: UI Requirements.
120. **AC-SL-120 (auto) — formula worked examples run as unit tests.** `autosave_due` (432 000 → 437 400: elapsed 5 400 ≥ 1 800, EVENT; latch at 438 700, due 439 200); `autosave_gate` (the FAIR, STRICT, cap and hard examples of F-2); `generation_select` (the F-3 example and its three variants); `save_verification` (the F-4 example, 63 284 B); `write_budget` (F-5: `main_hitch_ms` 5.2, `durable_ms` 20.8; HDD: 305.2, slack 29 700); `save_bytes_header` (F-6: `header_len` 1 800, `file_bytes` 63 284, header share 2.8 %; 128 blocks → 19 800); `loss_ticks` (F-7: 1 801, 19 800, 23 400, mean 7 200); `load_budget` (F-8: 128.7 ms and 146.1 ms); `ring_position` (F-9: `gen_next` 10, slot 1); `hex64` (F-10). The F-5 and F-6 constants (`H0` 600, `h_b` 150, `t_encode` 1.5 …) are test inputs, not measurements; the real writer's measured header size must fall in `H0` 500 – 700 and `h_b` 100 – 180 (measured under AC-SL-87). Cites: F-1 … F-10.

### Story-type and gate classification (for `/qa-plan`)

| Criteria | Story type | Required evidence | Gate (project default) |
|---|---|---|---|
| AC-SL-01 … 05, 07, 09 … 29, 31 … 57c, 61, 62, 64, 65, 68 … 81, 82, 83, 95a, 96 … 116, 118, 120, 123, 125, 126, 127 | Logic | automated test in `tests/unit/save-load/` or `tests/integration/save-load/`, file name `save_load_[feature]_test.gd` | BLOCKING |
| AC-SL-58 … 60, 82, 83 (with kernel, Graph and Controller doubles) | Integration | integration test or documented playtest | BLOCKING |
| AC-SL-06, 08 (lint half), 63, 86 (lint half), 95b | Config/Data (static) | CI lint pass | ADVISORY by default; recommend BLOCKING for AC-SL-06 and AC-SL-95b |
| AC-SL-30, 82b, 87 … 90 | Spike (performance and engine) | measurements in `production/qa/evidence/` | BLOCKING for the "save round-trip with version" spike exit |
| AC-SL-84, 91, 93 | Visual/Feel | retained screenshot plus lead sign-off | BLOCKING |
| AC-SL-92, 117 | UI | retained screenshot of each screen touched | BLOCKING |
| AC-SL-94 | Playtest | notes and questionnaire | ADVISORY for fairness; BLOCKING for "campaigns load" |
| AC-SL-66, 67, 84b, 85, 119b, 121, 122, 124 | Review | named reviewer | BLOCKED until their target sections or GDDs exist (see Gaps) |

`qa.level` and `testing.strict` overrides in `project.yaml`, if set, change the gate level of the first four rows only; at `qa.level: minimal` test evidence is waived but the UI and Visual/Feel screenshots are not.

### Gaps / untestable as written

- **Blocked on undesigned or in-flight GDDs (review criteria cannot pass yet).** AC-SL-66 and AC-SL-67 (the Controller/Body CONFLICT, OQ-9: the real-block G4 through disk is blocked, not passed); AC-SL-124 (no-clean-frame ordering, OQ-11); AC-SL-121 and AC-SL-122 are unblocked now that Attention, Layout and Body are designed, and AC-SL-96b's Suit and Sanity shapes follow the designed GDDs *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass)*; the `EVENT_TRIGGERS` names beyond `NEW_GAME` (OQ-24).
- **Blocked on the kernel owner.** The release-mode half of AC-SL-60 and the inside-`LOADING` compare of AC-SL-58 and AC-SL-59 depend on the verification-window mechanism K-4 (OQ-6); until it is answered the reproduction stage runs in the harness and in dev builds only.
- **Not decidable from the GDD, carried as Open Questions.** Behaviour of a knob set outside its safe range with no invariant violated — clamp, refuse or accept (OQ-28); the "target machine" definition for every (spike) criterion (OQ-34); the hold-to-confirm duration (OQ-32); hostile-payload decode allocation — the type walk runs after the decode allocates, so a 32 MiB nested payload may allocate far more than 32 MiB before the walk can refuse it; AC-SL-90 measures it and OQ-35 sets the ceiling, or the pre-walk moves to the raw bytes (ADR candidate); the physical encoding of a quarantine reason and the retire path (OQ-27).
- **Decided here and recorded (orchestrator rulings), so the criteria above are verifiable.** Backoff schedule (OQ-26); "session" = process lifetime (OQ-29); playtime = integer floor (OQ-30); duplicate-entropy `new_game` re-draws and never overwrites (OQ-31); the one-frame target applies to the main-thread sum `main_hitch_ms` (OQ-33); a block missing `default_state()` or a fixture factory is a failing AC-SL-65 test, not a boot refusal (OQ-36).
- **Not automatable by design.** "Imperceptible", "reads as a flash or a crash" and fairness are judged by people; AC-SL-91 and AC-SL-94 replace the adjectives with frame-time logs, stopwatch values and a questionnaire, and fairness is data, not a gate.

## Open Questions

Ids are stable and are cited from the sections above; rows are grouped (user decisions first, then amendments routed to other owners, then engine and ADR items, then author decisions recorded here) and never renumbered. "Owner" is who can answer; "Target" is where the answer lands. Nothing in this table is written as an existing rule of another GDD: routed items are requests, and every undesigned dependency is marked provisional wherever it appears.

**User decisions (informed by playtest).**

| # | Question | Owner | Target |
|---|---|---|---|
| OQ-1 | **The one question flagged for the user.** Should a repeated death restored from a **non-`RISKY`** generation step back to an older generation? The binding rule is "restart from the last autosave", so the default is the newest verified generation; step-back fires only when that generation is `RISKY` and the death came within `DEATH_LOOP_WINDOW_TICKS` of `T_downed` (SL-8, F-3). A non-`RISKY` loop (the same death on the same save, repeatedly) is possible; the cost is that the player may need to change what they do, which is the game. Instrument `deaths_per_restored_gen` and ask only if playtest shows players stuck | user (playtest) | playtest (AC-SL-51, AC-SL-94) |
| OQ-18 | A **pinned, main-menu-only manual slot** as the single fallback if the 3-person playtest finds Save & Quit plus autosave too punishing. Never an in-session save: save-anywhere makes a reload a free hallucination oracle (Pillar 4) and removes the cost of death | user (playtest) | playtest |
| OQ-19 | **Death-cost tuning**: `AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_TICKS`, `AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS` and the `LOSS_CEILING_TICKS` diagnostic; the loss window numbers 1 801 / 19 800 / 23 400 ticks and the 7 200-tick mean (`loss_ticks`, F-7). "If death turns into a quit, the knob is the interval, then the pinned slot, never save-anywhere". The measurable triggers (M1 to M3) and their named responses are in Edge Cases and AC-SL-94 *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)* | user (playtest) | playtest (AC-SL-55, AC-SL-94) |
| OQ-25 | **Accepted exposures**: a process kill or Alt-F4 rewinds at most `AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_TICKS + AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS` (18 000 ticks); Save & Quit then relaunch is a disguised retry — determinism means it buys a retry of inputs and, for up to one interval, a look at the physical world (SL-23). The trigger is no longer open-ended *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)*: metric **M2** `scout_death_rate` (> 0.25 of deaths within `SCOUT_DEATH_WINDOW_TICKS` 5 400 of first boarding a derelict — derelict-level at the MVP) and **M3** `quit_relaunch_rate` (> 0.30 of `QUIT` generations relaunched within `QUIT_RELAUNCH_WINDOW_S` 120 s, or 3 pairs in one 30-minute session) in Edge Cases, measured by AC-SL-94. First response: retune `AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_TICKS` once (OQ-19); a repeat trip, or M1 together with M2 or M3, escalates to Failure Contract & Re-wake (VS-A), never to deleting the `QUIT` generation (that would contradict SL-8). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass)* M2 uses derelict-level `T_enter` at the MVP: Web Map & Transit's `FIRST_BOARDED{derelict_id, T}` (the dock room, WM-28); Web Map does not supply compartment-level first entry, and a DIAGNOSTIC-only `FIRST_COMPARTMENT_ENTRY{room_id, T}` log by the Debug Overlay from Body section changes (never persisted) is the recorded refinement if M2 proves too coarse | user (playtest); Failure Contract; Web Map author for `FIRST_BOARDED` | playtest |

A generation captured while any of those is non-empty (reachable at a `RISKY` cap or a `QUIT`, since `CHANNELLING` is a soft gate state, or inside Assembly's one-tick pending window) verifies `NOT_REPRODUCED` in EXACT mode and halts a dev build. Resolution is the owners': clear the transient fields in `snapshot()` so the stored bytes equal the restored state, or move the clear out of restore. **Engine, spike and ADR items.**

| # | Question | Owner | Target |
|---|---|---|---|
| OQ-16 | **Engine [UNV] spike list** for the concept's "save round-trip with version": `var_to_bytes` int-exactness and signed `u64`; typed containers; `StringName` keys; Windows rename atomicity and target-exists behaviour; native SHA-256 throughput; flush and fsync; worker hand-off and thread file access; duplicate-key JSON behaviour. The engine reference has no file-I/O, serialization or hashing module (its `modules/` holds animation, audio, input, navigation, networking, physics, rendering, ui), so every claim beyond the two sourced ones is [UNV] | technical-director | spike (AC-SL-30, AC-SL-90) |
| OQ-17 | **Hash cost and the H64 ADR.** The kernel's D.11 assumed the hash is debug-only; the stored `state_hash` makes it a release cost on every capture and restore, and xxHash64 in GDScript may exceed 1.5 ms. Linked to OQ-2 | kernel ADR | ADR / spike (AC-SL-89) |
| OQ-23 | **Death-stub I/O at the `DEAD` tick.** The pre-check and staging read the file in the `DEAD` boundary; if `restart_ms` misses `LOADING_TARGET_MS`, a hot in-memory copy of the newest generation or pre-staging during the `DOWNED` window are the recorded alternatives (not MVP rules) | technical-director | spike (AC-SL-88) |

**Author decisions recorded here (orchestrator rulings made while closing the acceptance criteria; confirm or overturn).**

| # | Question | Owner | Target |
|---|---|---|---|
| OQ-26 | **Failure backoff schedule.** Ruled: retry delay after the n-th consecutive failure is `min(AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS × 2^(n−1), AUTOSAVE_FAIL_BACKOFF_MAX_TICKS)` = 300, 600, 1 200, 2 400, 4 800, 4 800 ticks, and the HUD notice is raised on failure `AUTOSAVE_FAIL_ESCALATE_COUNT` (3). Written into the knob row and asserted by AC-SL-25 | GDD (decided) | confirm at review |
| OQ-27 | **Encodings left to the ADR.** How a quarantined file "carries a reason" (suffix, sidecar or manifest entry) and the retire-campaign location are not specified; criteria assert them through host-local queries (`quarantine_reason(file)`, `list_saves()`) only | technical-director | ADR "atomic write, backup and thread model" |
| OQ-28 | **Knobs outside their safe range with no invariant violated** (for example `AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_TICKS` 100): clamp, refuse at boot, or accept with a diagnostic? The GDD states ranges but no enforcement for them; no criterion can be written until chosen. Recommended: refuse at boot with `SL_BOOT_KNOB_RANGE{knob}` for the tick and byte knobs, accept with a diagnostic for the enum knobs | GDD | next edit |
| OQ-29 | **"Session"** in `GATE_INPUT_ABSENT` ("logged once per session"). Ruled: process lifetime (a death-stub restore inside the same process does not log again) | GDD (decided) | confirm at review |
| OQ-30 | **`playtime` rounding.** Ruled: `T div TICK_RATE`, whole seconds, integer floor (7 290 s at `T = 437 400`) | GDD (decided) | confirm at review |
| OQ-31 | **Duplicate entropy in `new_game`.** Ruled: a `campaign_id` whose directory already exists is re-drawn from the entropy source and never overwrites | GDD (decided) | confirm at review |
| OQ-32 | **Hold-to-confirm duration.** The art bible fixes the form (linear bar) but gives no number; the value is the UX spec's, adjustable by accessibility options, and is recorded on the AC-SL-92 sheet | ux-designer | /ux-design |
| OQ-33 | **F-5 one-frame target.** Ruled: the "inside one frame" target applies to the main-thread sum `main_hitch_ms = capture_time_ms + t_encode + t_handoff` (≤ `SNAPSHOT_BUDGET_MS`); the component thresholds are tighter sub-limits (AC-SL-87) | GDD (decided) | spike |
| OQ-34 | **"The target machine"** for every (spike) criterion (AC-SL-30, 82b, 87, 88, 89): the minimum-spec CPU, storage type and OS are not named in the project's technical preferences | technical-director / producer | before the spike |
| OQ-35 | **Hostile-payload decode allocation.** The type walk (`MAX_DECODE_DEPTH`, `MAX_DECODE_NODES`) runs after `bytes_to_var` has allocated; a re-signed 32 MiB block of nested empty arrays may allocate far more than 32 MiB before the walk refuses it. Spike measures peak memory and time (AC-SL-90); proposed ceiling 8 × `MAX_BLOCK_BYTES`, or move the pre-walk to the raw bytes (ADR candidate) | technical-director | spike / ADR |
| OQ-36 | **Boot reasons for a missing `default_state()` or fixture factory.** Ruled: neither gets a new `SL_BOOT_*` reason; each is a failing AC-SL-65 test (a missing fixture factory fails the test, not the boot) | GDD (decided) | confirm at review |
